On fresh copies, this receiver identified the positive-phase fractions
of two mixtures within $[0.24469,0.25535]$ and $[0.74475,0.75541]$.
Using only those intervals and the charged endpoint counts of the two
pure standards, the observer predicted all 96 held endpoint bins at
exposure $3/16$, for two launch ports and both material charges.
Reversing the assignments missed 43 of 48 bins in each mixture.
The 99\% intervals account for all calibration and held counts together.
The forecast uses the affine mixing law for the two calibrated sources.
Vacuum has the same signed neutral response as one mixture with positive
fraction between 0.01236 and 0.01769. Its measured charged endpoints
miss that mixture's forecast in 46 of 48 bins. Keeping the two neutral
click rates separately identifies both phase fractions and the vacuum fraction
on the calibrated three-source family. The two boundary mixtures and one
held interior mixture pass all 144 charged endpoint comparisons at joint
99\% coverage. A geometric response to stress at the ordinary Newton
coupling requires further measurements. Source preparations,
counts and the replay are in
\path{model/experiments/interval_echo_einstein_station/field_stress_curvature/neutral_packet/}.

\subsection{Observers measuring quantum stress, volume and curvature}
\label{sec:quantum-small-ball-measurement}

A controlled six-mode quantum scalar in a periodic, diagonal Bianchi~I
box permits two independent measurements of the curvature associated with
changed matter. One spatial sine and cosine oscillator for each fundamental
axis wave vector give homogeneous mean stress. Orient a pair of number
excitations along different axes. Their initial total energy, spatial
metric and expansion rates coincide, while the pressures differ. The
generator evolves the quantum oscillators and the classical gravitational
momenta with one ADM Hamiltonian containing the matter-energy expectation.
The mass, action, metric variables and proper clock are inputs of this
regulated semiclassical comparison.

On separate copies, observers read energy and pressure from calibrated
field quadratures and reconstruct diagonal curvature from the return
times of three light signals. At one appointment the ground and one
excited source calibrate $\kappa$ and $\Lambda$. On other orientations,
an even-number mixture with the same stress as the odd preparation, and
later appointments, the largest held diagonal Einstein error is
$5.59\times10^{-6}$ in box units at the finest clock spacing.
Averaging away the pressure directions raises it to $0.00564$.
Suppressing backreaction gives $0.03323$. A reader initialized once
from the radar lengths and rates and three quantum quadratures predicts
the normalized round trips at two later appointments with maximum error
$9.99\times10^{-6}$. Exchanging the two most separated pressure
directions raises it to $0.00108$. The pure odd and even mixed states
have opposite measured parities and identical radar times.

\paragraph{Exact null balance in the homogeneous quantum comparison.}
\label{par:canonical-all-null}
Use log lengths $\log a_i$ with canonical momenta $\pi_i$, physical
volume $W=V_0 a_1a_2a_3$, expansion rates $H_i$, $\theta=\sum_i H_i$,
$C=\sum_{i<j}H_iH_j$ and $P=\sum_i p_i$. In the implemented diagonal
Hamiltonian the rates are $H_i=(\kappa/W)(\pi_i-\sum_j\pi_j/2)$, the
gravitational kinetic energy is $-WC/\kappa$, and the scalar force on
$\log a_i$ is $W p_i$. The canonical momentum update is therefore
$(\kappa/W)\dot\pi_i=-C-\Lambda+\kappa p_i$. Differentiating the rate,
including $\dot W/W=\theta$, gives
\[
 \dot H_i=(C+\Lambda)/2+\kappa(p_i-P/2)-\theta H_i .
\]
Substitution in the orthonormal Bianchi I spatial curvature gives
$G_{ii}=\kappa p_i-\Lambda$. The independent oscillator moment
equations give $\dot\rho=-\sum_i H_i(\rho+p_i)$. Consequently
$D=C-\Lambda-\kappa\rho$ satisfies $\dot D=-\theta D$ and $WD$ is
constant. The initial expansion sets $D=0$ and $W>0$ on the regular
solution interval. This proves $G_{00}=\Lambda+\kappa\rho$ throughout.
The standing-mode and diagonal metric symmetries set the off-diagonal
terms to zero. For every $k=(1,n)$ with $\sum_i n_i^2=1$, these
equations give $G(k,k)=\kappa T(k,k)$. Homogeneity in $k$ covers
arbitrary null normalization.

For the diagonal source each directional residual is a convex combination
of the three axial residuals with weights $n_i^2$. The maximum absolute
residual over the whole sky is attained on an axis. Table~\ref{tab:all-sky-radar}
gives that maximum on eleven held preparation--appointment pairs at each
spacing. The separate field and radar meters share the supplied proper
clock and homogeneous geometry. One fit of the ground and $x$-oriented
temporal readings at the first appointment sets the coupling at each
spacing. A second calculation uses the Hamiltonian coupling $1.6$ at
all spacings. The response survives this removal of the calibration fit.

\begin{table}[htbp]
\centering\small
\begin{tabular}{ccccc}
\hline
Radar spacing & Fitted $\kappa$ & Held, fitted & Held, fixed & Feedback off\\
\hline
0.08 & 1.601006 & $8.76\times10^{-5}$ & $1.07\times10^{-4}$ & 0.04113\\
0.04 & 1.600187 & $2.41\times10^{-5}$ & $2.77\times10^{-5}$ & 0.04111\\
0.02 & 1.600004 & $7.09\times10^{-6}$ & $7.16\times10^{-6}$ & 0.04111\\
\hline
\end{tabular}
\caption{Null residuals in the box units of the regulated semiclassical
Hamiltonian. The final column uses the three separate appointments of the
$y$-oriented control, with matter backreaction suppressed and the fitted
coupling retained in the readout. The entries are measured maxima. The
all-direction identity follows from diagonal symmetry; exact balance
along the regular flow follows from the Hamiltonian and scalar equations.}
\label{tab:all-sky-radar}
\end{table}

The ADM metric Hamiltonian and its matter coupling are inputs of this
comparison. On the native repair model, the
\hyperref[par:ray-pair-native-current]{opposite-current experiment}
tests a different pair of instruments. The algebra and constraint
propagation are formalized in
\path{Lean/ObserverPatchHolography/EinsteinBranch/RayPairBalance.lean}.
The archived quadrature counts, radar returns and analysis are in the
\href{https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/tree/main/code/matched_einstein_limit/quantum_scalar_radar}
{quantum scalar and radar comparison}.

\paragraph{Transfer between fields and gravitating cross correlations.}
In the same supplied classical geometry, prepare a second quantum
scalar and read the density and pressure of each field independently.
With the first-field coupling fixed, nine held radar estimates for the
second field give coupling ratios $0.999864$--$1.000109$. Halving its
gravitational weight yields $0.499882$--$0.500079$. Independent rim
areas at fixed proper volume give $1.001151$ and $0.500464$.
A second experiment supplies a bilinear energy density
$\lambda\langle AB\rangle$ between the two fields. At preparation,
opposite cross correlations have identical single-field density
matrices. With $\lambda$ measured by frequency splitting and gravity
calibrated on one field, the correlation predicts
$\Delta G_{00}=0.00296540$. Radar measures $0.00296837$, and rim
areas at equal volume give $0.00296947$. Removing the interaction
gives identical radar and rim records for the opposite correlations.
The comparison resolves an interaction contribution to stress on
this regulated Einstein source. The metric dynamics, oscillator
action and proper rulers enter its preparation. The spectra,
quadratures and geometry records are in
\path{code/matched_einstein_limit/quantum_scalar_radar/species_transfer/}.

\paragraph{An ordinary calibration applied to a seam correlation.}
\label{par:seam-gibbs-curvature}
Consider two preparations with the same full one-register density
matrices. On a finite tensor product with physical Hamiltonian $h$, use
a faithful joint thermal reference $\sigma=e^{-\beta h}/Z$, $\beta>0$.
For $K=-\log\sigma+\log(\sigma_A\otimes\sigma_B)$ the two marginal
logarithms and the scalar $\log Z$ cancel in the difference. Thus
$\Delta\langle K\rangle=\beta\Delta\langle h\rangle$ exactly, including
for coherent preparations. This argument applies to the two-field
interaction at a fixed initial geometry on a finite regulator. In the
oscillator calculation the independently measured covariance reads that
energy. A thermal reference is a separate choice of meter.

A realization uses the physical 99-state repair Hamiltonian on one
seam. Four eigenwords combine two memory magnitudes (0 and 2) with
two load words $(2,0)$ and $(1,1)$. In that order the energy rows are
$(0,0)$ and $(32,0)$ for the even reversal and positive coin phase.
Give each off-diagonal word probability $3/8$ and each diagonal word
$1/8$, then exchange these probabilities. The full individual states
are identical and the energy falls from 12 to 4. Blank memory with
vacuum and with one symmetric load gives a separate 0-to-8 calibration.

Embed the stationary seam as volume-independent internal energy
$E=\langle h\rangle$ in a homogeneous flat cell. With $q=\log a$,
proper volume $W=V_0e^{3q}$, conjugate momentum $p$ and unit lapse,
supply the gravitational Hamiltonian
\[
 \mathcal H=-\kappa p^2/(12W)+\Lambda W/\kappa+E .
\]
Its flow preserves the imposed initial constraint $\mathcal H=0$.
Hamilton's equations give $\dot q=-\kappa p/(6W)$ and
$2\ddot q=\Lambda-3\dot q^2$. Hence $W(3\dot q^2-\Lambda)=\kappa E$
and $G_{ii}=-\Lambda$ in the orthonormal frame. For every regular
time and every null direction $k=(1,\mathbf n)$, $|\mathbf n|=1$,
\[
 \Delta[W(G_{00}-\Lambda)]=\Delta[W G_{kk}]
 =\kappa\,\Delta\langle K\rangle/\beta .
\]
The two volumes are measured separately. The source prescription couples
one geometry to the expectation in each preparation.

Round-trip clocks on the two ordinary preparations give
$\kappa=1.60000105$. At three later appointments the measured temporal
differences are $12.799997$, $12.799988$ and $12.799973$. Joint
reference and source counts with an ordinary two-level thermometer give
an energy difference $7.95234\pm0.02032$ (one counting standard error),
against the population value 8. A control assigns gravitational charge
8 to all four words, keeping the matter Hamiltonian, counts and
ordinary calibration fixed. Every held geometric difference then
vanishes. The canonical metric and its coupling to the total energy
are explicit inputs. The raw tapes and exact native column checks
are in \path{code/matched_einstein_limit/seam_overlap/}.

At the common initial cell volume, compare the two seam states on
rest-frame balls of equal measured interior volume. Geodesic rim
areas and the population energy contrast 8 give coupling estimates
$1.62320,1.60575,1.60143$ on proper-volume radii $0.4,0.2,0.1$.
The estimate uses the leading geodesic-ball expansion. The higher
energy makes the smallest rim smaller by $4.32691\times10^{-6}$.
The regional energy forecast here weights the homogeneous density by
the ordinary diamond kernel. A separate spatial modular identification
is needed to apply it to a physical collar.

For an independently read geometry this coupling has a finite operator
test. At a faithful reference, write $Q$ for the derivative of the
volume-weighted temporal statistic, so a state tangent $X$ changes it
by $\operatorname{Tr}(QX)$. It agrees with the ordinary calibration on
every fixed-marginal tangent if and only if
$Q-\kappa h=A\otimes I+I\otimes B$. Indeed the tangents with both
partial traces zero are spanned by tensor products of traceless
Hermitian operators. Their trace-orthogonal complement is precisely
the space of the displayed local sums. On the four diagonal words
this requires $(Q_{01}+Q_{10}-Q_{00}-Q_{11})/4=8\kappa$.
The two ordinary observations alone leave that difference free.
On the free repair law another sector gives an exact obstruction.
Four private memory words act with energies $(0,0,33,32)$ times the
identity on every endpoint-load input. The same two probability
patterns differ by $1/4$ unit of Gibbs-measured energy when magnitude
and within-pair index are the two logical registers. Conditioned on
the word, material evolution differs only by a phase. Every read
confined to the remaining degrees of freedom gives identical outcome
probabilities. Repeating the preparation on $N$ such seams multiplies
the energy change by $N$ and preserves equality of all joint material
readouts. This sector tests two internal registers. For a spatial
collar the choice of regional source and geometric ruler carries
physical content.

\paragraph{A thermal buffer, an explicit signed error and its physical scale.}\label{par:thermal-transport-screen}
Partition the entire finite binary carrier into a core $A$, a buffer $B$ and
its exterior $C$. Let $\sigma$ be the faithful Gibbs reference of $H-\mu N$
at finite $\beta\ne0$. Whenever a port in $A$ is connected by hopping to a
port in $C$, the conditional mutual information $F=I_\sigma(A:C\mid B)$
is strictly positive for every finite buffer width. Indeed, equality in
strong subadditivity splits each central block of $B$ into left and right
factors on which the state is a product~\cite{hjpw}. Taking logarithms,
including the block probabilities, would give
$-\log\sigma=U_{AB}+V_{BC}$ with commuting summands. An exterior
occupation evolved by $H-\mu N$ would then depend only on the initial
$BC$ state. A vacuum and a single load launched at the core have
identical initial $BC$. At separation $d$, the one-load arrival
amplitude has leading term $i^d P_d t^d/(2^d d!)$, with $P_d>0$ the
number of shortest paths and hopping matrix element $-1/2$.
The vacuum gives zero at every exposure.

Replace the core by its marginal from an independent copy, giving
$\tau=\sigma_A\otimes\sigma_{BC}$. Mixing this arm into the original
reference has tangent $D=\tau-\sigma$. Write
$k_X=-\operatorname{Tr}(D_X\log\sigma_X)$. Exact entropy differences
and monotonicity of relative entropy give
\[
 k_{AB}-\beta\,\delta\langle H-\mu N\rangle =
 -F-D_{\rm rel}(\tau\Vert\sigma)
     +D_{\rm rel}(\tau_{AB}\Vert\sigma_{AB})\le -F<0 .
\]
On the twelve-port cell at $\beta=1/16$ put a seam in the core and
all six neighboring ports in the buffer. The full-cell response is
$1.15441086$ nats per unit replacement probability. The regional
response falls short by $0.000147181$, with $F=0.0000456504$ nats.
Removing exchange gives a classical nearest-neighbor model whose
conditional information vanishes exactly on this cut.

There is also an upper bound for an arbitrary traceless tangent $X$.
Put $k_Y=-\operatorname{Tr}(X_Y\log\sigma_Y)$ and
$L=k_{AB}+k_{BC}-k_B-k_{ABC}$. If $\sigma\pm tX$ are states, the
two signs in strong subadditivity give
$|L|\le F/t+t\operatorname{Tr}(X\sigma^{-1}X)$.
Indeed, subtract the four relative entropies in each entropy difference,
use data processing, and apply
$D_{\rm rel}(\rho\Vert\sigma)\le\log\operatorname{Tr}(\rho^2\sigma^{-1})$.
Consequently a sufficiently rapid decay of the signed error could
supply the macroscopic estimate on a specified family. The required
ordinary gain has an independent scale test. A homogeneous smooth
energy perturbation on a three-ball requires modular response per
physical energy $2\pi\ell/5$ to leading order. A gain common to radii
$\ell$ and $2\ell$ incurs at least $1/3$ relative error on one of
them, since $\max(|x-1|,|x/2-1|)\ge1/3$. Thus a locally thermal
bulk with one uniform gain needs an additional source of radius
dependence to realize (G2). The finite measurements, bounds and
preparation conventions are in
\path{code/matched_einstein_limit/source_implication/}.

\paragraph{Radar on the freely formed overlap.}
On an 80-cell federation a moving excess prepares two correlated heads.
Four material stations register first arrivals and returns with an
independent exchange clock, including every missed appointment. For a
static reciprocal distance and arbitrary station clock gains the product
of the three clockwise ranges divided by the three counterclockwise ranges
must equal one. At a quarter-unit sampling interval and six-unit horizon,
one triangle gives $0.74916$ with an excess and $1.30429$ with a vacancy.
Releasing an equal mixture and discarding the species label before
computing the clock statistic cancels the directional asymmetry at
leading shortest-path order. A held triangle gives $0.89651$ at
finite exposure. Even after fitting station gains, one directed range
must change by at least $1.8\%$ to fit a reciprocal metric on this panel.
For a window of three short ticks the directional asymmetry first
appears at sixth order on a tested triangle, with a strictly signed
coefficient certified from the formed state. Simply shortening a
fixed number of ticks makes all the censored ranges approach one
half of the elapsed horizon. Five distinct stations would then have
equal normalized separations, which require four Euclidean dimensions.
Removing read-stage contrast restores reciprocity in the measured
control. These tests exclude
an exact static interpretation of the measured finite-arrival statistic.
A common proper metric inferred by another instrument, or approached
with controlled errors under refinement, is a distinct physical
identification. The formation, calibration, misses and held cycles are
in \path{model/experiments/interval_echo_einstein_station/collar_pairing/ranging/}.

A coherent probe on the same freely formed head source supplies a
separate phase ruler. Four launch phases on bare ports 2 and 4 recover
the interference between their transmission amplitudes at each
receiver. Holding the two one-head marginals fixed and changing their
pairing gives different ratios of excess to vacancy phase response on
baselines $0$--$1$ and $0$--$38$. A second source variation changes
ordinary reversal energy. Even allowing each species its own
source-dependent wavenumber and each baseline a static phase offset,
a common length on each baseline requires a vanishing determinant
between these two variations. The exact-law determinant lies between
$5.05497721$ and $5.05497727$ in units of $10^{-5}$. The
\href{https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/tree/main/model/experiments/interval_echo_einstein_station/collar_pairing/fringe_ruler}{fringe measurements}
allow path-dependent refraction and diffraction. Extracting proper
length would require an independent calibration of those effects.

Five material clocks also give a centered spatial difference on the
formed source. In the spherical comparison chart for ports
$(0,1,38,39,5)$, four positive weights $w_i$ sum to one and cancel
the tangent displacements $v_i$ about port 0. For any scalar
detector record $f$, the reading
$2(\sum_iw_i f_i-f_0)/\sum_iw_i|v_i|^2$ cancels every affine
field. All eigenvalues of its directional second moment lie between
$0.29$ and $0.40$ after trace normalization. Independent four-unit
return counts give positive spatial second differences in the
pairing response, $0.003414$ for vacancies and $0.003681$ for excesses
per squared radar unit. Calibration against an ordinary half-unit
exchange write assigns them respectively $2.85$ and $0.163$ equivalent
energy units. Under a change from independent to formed heads, the
affine transport fitted to the first four positions misses the fifth
by $0.00734$ radar units. The
\href{https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/tree/main/code/matched_einstein_limit/source_implication/full_geometry/station_balls/reciprocal_screen/range_contraction/formed_ranges/affine_stencil}{five-clock comparison}
uses all five stations to select the spherical chart. Refinement and an
independent proper ruler would be needed to identify a spacetime
curvature from this finite material observable.

\paragraph{Increasing entropy and decreasing volume on the same source.}
Let $\rho$ be the two formed head coins, $\pi$ the product of their
marginals, and $\rho_s=(1-s)\pi+s\rho$. An unread sharp sign
measurement applied with probability $2u$ puts a fraction $u$ of the
left reversal states in the odd sector. The full stored state on its
support is $\operatorname{diag}(1-u,u)\otimes\rho_s$. Its ordinary
exchange energy is $16+u$ and its entropy in nats is
$H_2(u)+S(\rho_s)$. Changing $s$ preserves the two coin marginals
and the parked energy. A fresh material probe returning to one unmarked
port is sensitive to every two-coin observable on the even source.
The return effects span the sixteen real Hermitian components on
that subspace. The parity weights here follow from the independent
sharp sign preparation.

A separate clock survey on copies of the same source measures twelve
directed distances. Fitting station gains and symmetrizing gives a
tetrahedral volume $V$ and area $A$ from Gram determinants. On six
clock menus and nine interior preparations adding exchange energy
decreases $V$ in every case. At $u=1/4$, $s=1/2$, with quarter-unit
ticks and horizon six, the volume derivative is $-0.059832$.
Yet the derivative of the area of the unit-volume shape $A/V^{2/3}$
is $+0.022585$, and the entropy derivative is $+\log 3$. Both
derivatives are positive on all 54 comparisons. A shrinking
reconstructed volume alone therefore supplies no stationarity identity.

An independent preparation control matches the original survey volume
on both an ordinary change and a held change of the correlations.
In the even and odd sectors use coin pairing strengths $s+c/(1-u)$
and $s-c/u$. The combined coin state is $\rho_s$. At $c=0$ the
entropy derivative in $c$ vanishes. On each of the 54 settings the
volume derivative in $c$ is nonzero. Adjust $c$ using the measured
volume alone. At $u=1/4,s=1/2$, half ticks and horizon six, adding
one differential unit of ordinary energy at fixed mean coin state gives
$(dA,dS)=(+0.05794670,+1.09861229)$ at fixed volume. Strengthening
pairing at fixed ordinary energy gives $(+0.0000440662,-0.03848196)$
at the same reference and volume. The first pair would require
$\eta=-18.9590$. Even using that value leaves a signed residual
of $-0.0393174$ nats for the held change. The ordinary calibration
is negative in all 54 comparisons. The adjuster changes correlations
between the two preparation choices. Finite trials on either side
use admissible probabilities for the original sharp sign measurement.

The original distances also permit a directly matched volume. At
half-unit ticks and horizon six, vary $s$ near $1/4$ and adjust $u$
near $1/8$ to hold the observed volume fixed. At the central
preparation, $dA/ds=+0.00075879$ and $dS/ds=+0.00281940$ along this
curve. Finite changes from $s=0.2421875$ to $0.2578125$ increase
area by $0.000011856$ and entropy by $0.000044065$ nats at measured
volume $2.773791400$. No positive boundary entropy coefficient cancels
the two signed terms here. These numerical population comparisons test
the stored heads and this particular tetrahedron. The directed ranges
have a measured reciprocity defect. Establishing an intrinsic algebra
and proper ball on which the cancellation holds requires independent
space and stress readings.

A different entropy record uses the balanced outcome of a completed
return. Ordinary calibration then has the positive sign. On the same
six clock menus the area response to pairing supplies between
$0.0982\%$ and $0.6012\%$ of the magnitude required by this record.
Allowing positive mixtures of twelve bridge settings and nondecreasing
combinations of the six shapes raises the upper bound to $3.059\%$.
The ordinary and paired changes have different spatial distributions.
The test concerns a scalar reduction on this apparatus.
The preparation, full probabilities and geometry calculation are in
\path{code/matched_einstein_limit/source_implication/}.

\paragraph{A preparation with stationary entropy.}
Correlating the preparation choices changes this result in a measurable
way. At half-unit ticks, horizon six, odd probability $1/4$ and mean
pairing $1/2$, choose $c=-0.0046509709$. This corresponds to sharp
sign read probabilities $0.48139612$ on independent heads and
$0.51860388$ on formed heads. The fixed-volume ordinary change
gives $(dA,dS)=(+0.05794816,-1.80510754)$, and the fixed-energy
pairing change gives $(+0.0000440470,-0.0013720818)$. Both use
$\eta=31.15039$. The two changes span the constant-volume surface
in this three-parameter family. In the coordinates given by odd,
formed and joint probabilities, the two constrained Hessian
eigenvalues are $-0.37125$ and $-0.06002$. Six finite neighbouring
preparations at the same measured volume have smaller $S+\eta A$.

The gain can also be fixed on a separate preparation. Conditional
sharp-read probabilities $31/64$ and $33/64$ with equally likely
independent and formed heads give $\eta=23.10424$ on the matched
ordinary-energy tangent. Holding that gain and an independently
chosen target volume, constrained maximization gives a second source.
Its odd and formed probabilities are 0.248741 and 0.401402. On
two finite matched-volume comparisons the measured compensation
fractions are 0.99999984 for ordinary exchange and 0.99999855 for
pairing. These figures test the construction of a selected stationary
state. The matching operation on the correlated source changes both
the reversal energy and its correlation with the two coins.

An additional source variation distinguishes this restricted maximum
from physical entanglement equilibrium. For a fixed half-tick,
horizon-six menu, twelve directed mean ranges are twelve linear
effects on a faithful four-dimensional coin state. There is a
traceless Hermitian variation that leaves every range fixed. The
formed ensemble can realize a sufficiently small change in this
direction by reading the final address of the mobile load and using
an address-dependent lottery of original or independently formed
standby heads. All trials contribute. At the first stationary
source above the even-block entropy slope along the unit variation
is $+0.017034$ nats. The undephased formed arm has positive weight.
Changing that arm leaves both the complete radar geometry of this
menu and the ordinary energy fixed, while changing the entropy.
At the second, separately calibrated source the same change in the
never-dephased formed arm gives a full-head slope exceeding $0.00300$
nats per unit of the normalized component variation, with the same
twelve ranges and ordinary energy fixed. Other clock appointments
can distinguish the change. Thus the
native menu and three preparation controls give a finite maximum;
admitting additional native transfers exposes a missing response.
The source selection, finite differences and effect certificates
are in \path{code/matched_einstein_limit/source_implication/}.

\paragraph{Equilibrium on the complete finite head algebra.}
Enlarging the state search gives a stronger positive test. Let $R_i$
be the twelve Hermitian range effects, with $r_i=\operatorname{Tr}
(\chi R_i)$. The independently calculated tetrahedral area $A(r)$
and volume $V(r)$ define operators $\widehat A=\sum_i A_{,i} R_i$ and
$\widehat V=\sum_i V_{,i}R_i$. At a faithful state with regular
volume level, stationarity on every trace-preserving, volume-preserving
variation is equivalent to
\[
 -\log\chi+\eta\widehat A-\lambda\widehat V=\gamma I.
\]
Indeed the trace and volume functionals span the annihilator of their
common kernel. The operator identity makes the first entropy derivative vanish along
every variation invisible to all twelve ranges at that state.

Retain the separately calibrated $\eta=23.1042362$ and measured
volume $2.76347184$. Solving the normalized matrix exponential
of $\eta\widehat A-\lambda\widehat V$, with its range means
calculated self-consistently, gives odd weight $0.20243684$
and volume multiplier $51.08864$. The gradient residual across
all 31 traceless parity-block coordinates is
$2.42\times10^{-13}$ in Hilbert--Schmidt norm. The largest
eigenvalue of the unconstrained Lagrangian Hessian is $-3.4361$.
The prior twelve-range-invisible change gives entropy slope below
$1.4\times10^{-15}$ in magnitude. A separate visible, volume-matched
change gives $dS=-0.632510$ and $dA=+0.0273764$.
The clock effects preserve reversal parity. A coherence between
parity sectors is invisible to them, and parity dephasing increases
entropy whenever such a coherence is present. The full first-order
test therefore includes all 63 traceless directions on this
eight-dimensional support. Positive mixtures of prepared local
Pauli eigenstates and an unread native sign measurement realize the
two required blocks on the finite apparatus. The preparation uses the
selected equilibrium weights.

An interval certificate establishes existence at the actual finite
calibration. Bounded propagation of the native integer generators
and outward rounding enclose the independently measured gain in
$[23.1042361746,23.1042362179]$ and the target volume in
$[2.76347183678870,2.76347183678880]$. Throughout larger enclosing
parameter intervals the entropy Hessian dominates the geometric
curvature on a neighbourhood of the reported state. A feasible
interior point and a strict objective gap at the neighbourhood
boundary give an interior constrained maximum. Its preparation
weights have strictly positive slack. This proves existence for
the finite survey and support.

This preparation is the unique global maximum over all density
matrices on the eight-dimensional support at the prescribed survey
volume. Write $X_*$ for the localized solution, $Y$ for any other
feasible state, $D=Y-X_*$ and $T=\eta A-\lambda V$ at the true
multiplier. The Gibbs identity gives
\[
 (S+\eta A)(Y)-(S+\eta A)(X_*)=
 -D_{\rm rel}(Y\|X_*)+T(Y)-T(X_*)-dT_{X_*}(D).
\]
The relative entropy in nats is at least $\|D\|_1^2/2$ by quantum
Pinsker~\cite{carlen2016pinsker}. The twelve exact Hermitian range effects have certified
spectral endpoints $l_i,u_i$, giving
$|\operatorname{Tr}(R_i D)|\le (u_i-l_i)\|D\|_1/2$.
A 64-box interval cover of their full spectral rectangle bounds
$|d^2T(D,D)|\le 0.72\|D\|_1^2$ throughout every state segment.
For this bound the squared triangle areas and squared tetrahedral
volume are polynomials in the six squared edges. Each squared edge
is a monomial in the twelve positive raw ranges. Expanding these
finite sums in log ranges bounds their values and both derivatives
before taking the square roots. All five squared measures have
positive lower bounds on each box.
Taylor's formula gives a global objective gap of at least
$0.14\|Y-X_*\|_1^2$, including singular states and coherences.
The verification is in \texttt{full\_algebra/global\_survey.py}
in the source-implication package.

The same measured volume also admits an explicit nonstationary preparation.
Use the diagonal product-state lottery
$W(z)=I_4/32+(7/8)(z|10\rangle\langle10|+(1-z)|11\rangle\langle11|)$
and perform the unread sign write with probability $2q$. The
parity blocks are $(1-q)W$ and $qW$. At $q=1/64$ the target volume lies
between the readings at $z=0.79363406$ and $0.79363408$,
with $\partial_z V<-0.031$. Varying $q$ and adjusting $z$ at fixed
volume gives $7.47<d(S+\eta A)/dq<7.49$ nats per unit odd fraction,
from the range effects and $S=H_2(q)+S(W)$.
Both preparations use the same carrier, observer survey and ordinary
calibration. Selecting the optimizing preparation is a physical
condition separate from specifying the observed volume.

There is an ensemble preparation procedure without the solution as an
input. At fixed $\lambda$, replace a trial state $X$ by
$B(X)=\exp(\nabla T(X))/\operatorname{Tr}\exp(\nabla T(X))$.
For $U=B(X)$ and $W=B(Y)$ the sum of the two relative entropies is
$\operatorname{Tr}[(U-W)(\nabla T(X)-\nabla T(Y))]$. Pinsker bounds
it below by $\|U-W\|_1^2$. The absolute-entry curvature certificate
bounds it above by $0.72\|U-W\|_1\|X-Y\|_1$. Thus tracial iteration
converges to the unique fixed point. Comparing the global Lagrangian
maxima at two prices shows that their measured volumes decrease
weakly with price. The endpoint volumes bracket the independent
volume target. Bisection and iteration yield $\lambda=51.0886428$
and the positive native preparation weights. This uses the material
survey and a classical ensemble controller. Passive evolution of one
source has a different selection question.

The same result applies to any number $N$ of disjoint copies of this
survey. For a joint state $\Omega$ with marginals $Y_i$, the observers
may add the individually measured areas at fixed total volume
$\sum_i V(Y_i)=NV_0$, or pool the twelve mean ranges at fixed
$V(\bar Y)=V_0$, where $\bar Y=N^{-1}\sum_i Y_i$. In the latter
case the total area assigned by this convention is $NA(\bar Y)$.
In both conventions the joint objective drops from its product maximum
by at least $0.28D_{\rm rel}(\Omega\|X_*^{\otimes N})$.
Indeed the one-copy Lagrangian gap before fixing volume is at least
$0.28D_{\rm rel}(Y\|X_*)$ by the curvature bound and Pinsker.
For separate surveys sum these inequalities and add the correlation
entropy $\sum_i S(Y_i)-S(\Omega)$. For a pooled survey use the
one-copy inequality at $\bar Y$ and add $NS(\bar Y)-S(\Omega)$.
The volume terms cancel and the relative-entropy chain identity gives
the bound, including for entangled states. Repeating the nonstationary
preparation also preserves its nonzero derivative per copy exactly
at every $N$. These macroscopic record banks measure fixed-size
tetrahedra. Repeated readings alone supply no shrinking proper balls.

\paragraph{Does a connected survey select this state?}
Parked heads evolve with $H=16I+Q$, where $Q$ measures the odd
reversal sector. Every parity-block density is stationary on the
unit-filled board. A simple repeated drive supplies a distinct,
fully calculable relaxation. Launch a vacancy or excess uniformly
on the $M=960$ native ports, allow a dwell $t=1/16$ or a quiet
cycle with equal probabilities, and discard the probe at every exit.
For each fixed parity write $h=2H+cI$, $U=e^{-ith/2}$ and
$\Phi(X)=\operatorname{Tr}_{\rm port}(U(I_M\otimes X)U^\dagger)/M$.
This unital channel is a Hilbert--Schmidt contraction. On traceless
head matrices the exact Gram form $\|[h,I_M\otimes X]\|_2^2$ has
least eigenvalue 256 for each charge and parity. All native row sums
in absolute value are at most 43, so
$\|[U,I_M\otimes X]\|_2\ge(t/4)\|[h,I_M\otimes X]\|_2$.
Expanding $\|(X+\Phi(X))/2\|_2^2$ therefore gives a contraction
factor in squared norm of at most $1-1/61440$.
The limiting blocks are $(1-p)I_4/4$ and $pI_4/4$, with $p$
the conserved odd weight. Zero squared-contrast gain on the same
graph gives the identity channel in even parity.

One such limit matches the calibrated tetrahedral volume, at
$0.10750779<p<0.10750781$. It has $\langle Z\otimes Z\rangle=0$,
whereas the variational optimum gives $0.460895$.
Add $(aY\otimes Z+bZ\otimes Z)/2$ to the even block, using Pauli
matrices. Both individual marginals and ordinary energy are fixed.
Since $\partial_b V>0.03539$, a local $b(a)$ holds the volume exactly.
At its origin $-0.004816<d(S+\eta A)/da<-0.004815$.
Thus attaining the specified volume by physical relaxation on a single
connected board permits a nonstationary response in a correlation
that neither head reads alone.

There is also a test using the same radar as the original equilibrium
experiment. Keep the two heads after a complete capped survey on one
of the twelve directed routes. All missed arrivals and timeouts are
included. For both probe charges, on every route the expectation of
left-coin $X$ at the prepared optimum decreases by at least $0.000519$.
Thus any fixed head-independent lottery over these 24 settings changes
the optimum on reuse. Fresh copies and reused heads implement different
experiments. The complete channel and its propagation bounds are in
\path{source_implication/full_algebra/reuse/}.

With equal, independently scheduled reuse the full channel contracts
traceless Hilbert--Schmidt differences by a factor below $0.92$ in
each parity. This follows from exact positive pivots of
$0.918^2 I-\widehat J^{\mathsf T}\widehat J$ for the rounded
15-coordinate matrix, with the trace-norm channel error transferred
to the Hilbert--Schmidt bound. A fixed-point residual encloses the
two stationary densities. Their mixture matches the calibrated
volume for exactly one odd fraction, between 0.1340 and 0.1346.
Its joint $Z\otimes Z$ reading lies between 0.12435 and 0.12459.
At the balanced source the same mean exceeds 0.46089. For any source
at the same survey volume, the deficit in entropy plus calibrated
area is at least $0.14$ times its squared trace-norm distance to the
maximizer.
The difference of these means is a lower bound on that distance, so
the attracting source misses the maximum by more than 0.01583 nats.
After 256 uniform cycles, independent simulated cohorts gave 9,336
positive products from the reused source and 11,983 from the optimum,
each in 16,384 launches. These counts alone give a lower bound above
0.01011 nats at simultaneous $99\%$ coverage with the settling and
numerical allowances included.\footnote{\href{https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/tree/main/code/matched_einstein_limit/source_implication/full_algebra/reuse/selection_test}{Independent product reading, finite area and entropy differences, and population certificate.}}

Two source variations demonstrate the loss of stationarity.
Vary the even-block coefficients of $Y\otimes Z/2$ and $X\otimes Z/2$,
each compensated by $Z\otimes Z/2$ to keep volume fixed. Both
marginals and the ordinary energy stay fixed. The gains $-dS/dA$
would have to lie respectively in $[12.28,16.07]$ and $[18.35,24.73]$.
The area derivatives have opposite signs. A combination cancels area
and volume while leaving entropy derivative below $-0.00033$ nats per
unit $X\otimes Z/2$ coefficient. The nonzero volume derivative of
$Z\otimes Z/2$ and nonzero volume-matched area derivative of
$Y\otimes Z/2$ make the two-constraint Jacobian invertible. The
implicit function theorem therefore keeps both readings exactly
constant on a physical local path. The native product-lottery
margins exceed 0.20. A correction depending only on this measured
area, volume, ordinary energy and the two individual head states
is constant along the path.

The full directed means give a stronger test. In the eighteen
parity-resolved correlation coordinates that preserve both marginals,
the twelve range effects have rank twelve. A rational right inverse
with residual norm below $2.44\times10^{-7}$ corrects a proposed
direction to an exact common null vector $D$ of all twelve rows.
The fixed-point enclosure and its matrix logarithm bound the entropy
slope along $D$ between $1.759\times10^{-5}$ and $5.746\times10^{-5}$
nats per unit control. Since each range is affine in the prepared
state, every mean stays fixed along the straight line $\rho+tD$.
At $t=10^{-7}$ the reachable state has entropy greater by at least
$1.55\times10^{-12}$ nats. A separately readable, parity-blind
$Y\otimes Y$ coincidence distinguishes the two states. This
excludes compensation by any function of the twelve mean ranges
and the preserved marginals on this source. The original time bins retain the information lost by averaging.

Six additional joint coin readings suffice to recover the stored
entropy. Keep the parity weight and each one-head marginal in each
parity sector fixed. On independent copies measure the Pauli products
$XZ,YX,YY,YZ,ZY,ZZ$, with the first letter acting on the left coin.
These six scalar means and the twelve directed mean ranges determine
all eighteen remaining correlation coordinates. A rational approximate
inverse gives a certified smallest singular value above
$9.8966\times 10^{-9}$ for the physical response matrix.
The six-dimensional kernel of the range means proves minimality for
additional scalar linear readings on this slice. The reconstruction
uses population means. Its certified upper bound on error amplification
is $1.011\times10^8$ from Euclidean readout error to
Hilbert--Schmidt state error.

The direction $D$ was selected from entropy and ranges alone.
Independent four-tick measurements of the full marked force on this
same source give strictly positive slopes along it. In native force
units per source coefficient their enclosures are
$[1.25430,1.25464]\times10^{-8}$ for vacancy and
$[4.89110,4.89128]\times10^{-7}$ for excess. The eight calibrated
delayed local readings also distinguish the transfer in population.
Thus the discrepancy survives an independently defined material
measurement. Identifying that force with regional null stress,
or the twelve ranges with a proper-ball curvature, requires an
additional source map.

A different direction preserves the two independently calibrated material
forces as well as all twelve mean ranges. The fourteen linear effects
have rank fourteen on the eighteen correlation coordinates. Their
common kernel contains a native direction whose entropy derivative
lies in $[1.1964,4.7078]\times10^{-5}$ nats per unit coefficient.
A step of $10^{-7}$ raises entropy by more than $1.004\times10^{-12}$
nats while preserving all fourteen readings exactly. Thus adding
these two force means to the geometry leaves the stationarity
obstruction in place.

The original radar does contain enough information to reconstruct
the full stored state. Record, for each directed launch, the fraction
of all launches returning at each half-tick before the cutoff. Keep
the original trial denominator and average over the two equally
likely probe charges. Thirty-one of these probabilities determine
the normalized state on $M_4\oplus M_4$, including both marginals
and the odd parity weight. A certified affine inverse bounds the
Euclidean error amplification by $1.576\times10^7$. No second
head instrument is needed. As a finite counting bound, $2^{72}$
independent launches per route on each of two preparations give
simultaneous 99 percent entropy errors below $0.03328$ nats.
A union bound on bin frequencies, the calibrated inverse, projection
onto positive normalized block states and the dimension-eight
entropy continuity bound~\cite{audenaert07} give this result. It
distinguishes a pure stored product from its unread sign-dephased
twin, whose entropy is $\log 2$. A transfer invisible to all
twelve means changes the return probability at tick ten from the
third to the fourth station.

\paragraph{A common spatial volume read from one link.}
The four stations have an operational geometry on every normalized
head preparation. Score a completed round trip by its duration, give
each timeout the capped range 3, and average fairly over charge and
launch orientation. For the six pairs of ports $0,1,38,39$, the
orientation-averaged timeout probabilities exceed
$0.630,0.993,0.999,0.836,0.994,0.725$ respectively for either charge.
Thus each length lies between three times its lower bound and 3.
For squared lengths $L_{ij}^2$, the anchored Gram matrix has entries
$(L_{0i}^2+L_{0j}^2-L_{ij}^2)/2$. At each of the 64 vertices of
this box its least eigenvalue exceeds $1.55$. Affineness gives that
bound throughout, and concavity of the logarithmic determinant gives
tetrahedral volume greater than $1.57$ cubed range units. This proves
a nondegenerate three-dimensional Euclidean realization for these four
stations, unique up to rigid motion and reflection.

One observer using only $38\to1$ can also forecast this survey.
For a known probe charge the 36 ordered pairs of first-relay and
return appointments through appointment nine determine all 31 normalized
head coordinates. Independent calibration fixes six linear scores on
these local probabilities. Their uniform population error over all
head states is below $4.72\times10^{-11}$ range units for vacancy
and $1.22\times10^{-9}$ for excess. Separate propagation on three
preparations yields volumes $2.752409$, $2.732611$ and $2.747317$.
Retaining first-relay hits on failed round trips gives full rank by
appointment eight on every directed route. If the charge is hidden
and correlated with the head state, the joint four-block state has
63 coordinates and is recovered by appointment eleven. The inverse
calibration is sensitive to counting error. With $2^{72}$ simulated
launches per preparation and charge, both local readers distinguish
the first two volumes at simultaneous 99 percent coverage across all
36 length comparisons. On the excess reader the respective volume
intervals lie inside $[2.75120,2.75353]$ and $[2.73144,2.73387]$.

Raw durations place a stronger restriction on a common ruler.
Through the volume-matched stationary preparation there is a reachable
line preserving all twelve pooled mean ranges and both independent
marked forces. The stored entropy increases on the line. On the same
link $1\leftrightarrow38$, in each orientation separately, the
probability of completing by each of appointments 2 through 12
strictly increases for vacancy and strictly decreases for excess.
Summation by parts shows that every nondecreasing duration score with
a positive increment there gives opposite signs for the two length
changes. Species-specific increasing recalibrations preserve this
obstruction. The calibrated common ruler instead uses the joint relay
and return records to predict the independently fixed six-edge survey.
A proper-ball interpretation requires compatible measurements on
nested regions, their boundary areas, and a common transported clock.

There is a quantitative condition on embedding this experiment in
a larger ball. Suppose it contains $N$ disjoint measured bridge
regions $R_i$ reproducing the same marginal change and an additional
system $E$. Put $U=R_1\cdots R_N$, $I=\sum_iS(R_i)-S(U)$ and
$H=S(E\mid U)$. The identity $S(UE)=\sum_i S(R_i)-I+H$ holds for
arbitrarily correlated states. A finite generalized-entropy
residual $|\Delta S(UE)+\eta\Delta A|\le\epsilon$ therefore requires
\[
 \Delta I-\Delta H-\eta\Delta A
 \;\ge\; N(4.93893\times10^{-7})-\epsilon
\]
in nats for the tested displacement. For first variations the
corresponding coefficient exceeds $4.94001\times10^{-5}$ nats
per unit source change. Independent copies with no additional
degrees of freedom and the same fixed range geometry accumulate
the discrepancy. A connected proper-ball refinement could change
the area, total correlation or conditional entropy. Its own
measurements must supply the compensating term.

Even an arbitrary correlation-creating perturbation cannot supply
this first-order compensation at a faithful product reference
$\bigotimes_i\rho_i\otimes I_E/d_E$. The negative logarithm there
is the sum of the local negative logarithms, each extended by
identities, plus $(\log d_E)I$. The latter pairs to zero with every
normalized state derivative. The first law therefore gives
$\delta S(UE)=\sum_i\delta S(R_i)$ for every differentiable
perturbation at that reference. Enlarging unused maximally mixed
capacity this way preserves the measured first-order discrepancy.

Let $p(\sigma)=o+A x(\sigma)$ be this invertible clock map and
let $g$ be the source-coordinate covector of any independently
calibrated head observable. The time-bin score $w=A^{-T}g$ gives its
change as $w^T[p(\sigma)-p(\rho)]$. For a faithful reference
$\rho$, taking the observable $-\log\rho$ gives the exact identity
\[
 S(\sigma)-S(\rho)=w^T[p(\sigma)-p(\rho)]-D(\sigma\Vert\rho).
\]
The first variation therefore has a clock readout. Taking instead
the separately measured, four-tick translation force yields an
absolute force prediction for either carrier on every normalized
state in the two-block algebra. The Hamiltonian and independent
material calibration fix the two sets of weights. The frozen scores
have uniform absolute errors below $2.34\times10^{-14}$ for vacancy
and $5.95\times10^{-14}$ for excess, in native force units at
population precision. On three held product preparations the six
forecasts from simulated clock counts cover the independent force
readings at simultaneous 99 percent confidence, with $2^{48}$
synthetic trials per preparation and directed route. A curvature
prediction would additionally require clocks defining proper balls
and a geometric response at the ordinary-matter coupling. The
finite observation matrices, readout weights and held trials are in
\path{source_implication/full_algebra/reuse/}.

At the deliberately prepared variational maximum, two independent
transfers test the signs of the physical responses. An
unread sharp sign measurement on the left adds ordinary exchange
energy. In a different arm, supplying the left and right heads
from independent copies weakens their correlation at fixed ordinary
energy and preserves each head's marginal. A unitary rotation of
the right coin about its measured marginal axis can match the
survey volume: it preserves energy, entropy and both head marginals.
The appointment of the right rotation uses volume alone. Per unit
probability of the ordinary read the numerical derivatives are
\[
 (\delta E,\delta S,\delta A|_V)
   =(+0.297563,+0.409453,-0.0177220).
\]
For the correlation transfer they are respectively
$0,+0.231106,-0.0100028$. Thus a source can have identical
individual head records while a change in the joint records carries
entropy and a signed area deficit. Both measured ratios
$-\delta S/(\delta A|_V)$ are $23.1042362$ at the selected state.
At the exact maximizer, interval propagation places the two entropy
derivatives in $[0.409426,0.409481]$ and $[0.231079,0.231134]$.
The rotation changes volume with derivative between
$-2.587\times10^{-5}$ and $-2.564\times10^{-5}$. A locally
volume-preserving rotation adjustment therefore exists for each arm,
and both matched area slopes are strictly negative. Finite one-sided
transfers at matched volume converge toward these values. The exchange energy here concerns the parked heads and the
area belongs to their specific clock survey.

\paragraph{Independent source and directional readings.}
A sharp terminal click after a freely launched excess interrogates the
same stored state. At a single unmarked port the return-effect derivatives
through order 40 contain a $32\times32$ integer minor with determinant
184 modulo 1009. The population curve therefore distinguishes the two
complete parity blocks, including their relative weight. Switching off
the squared contrast during interrogation reduces the rank to five.
A separate finite-count experiment used 512 single-terminal settings
and $2^{52}$ synthetic trials per setting and bank. The inferred
ordinary and pairing modular changes were respectively
$0.40945\pm0.07935$ and $0.23111\pm0.07935$ nats, with simultaneous
99\% sampling bounds also covering the two energy contrasts. The energy
meter inferred changes of $0.29756325$ and $-0.00000004$, each within
$0.00000072$ in these units. The inference uses a reference bank and
separate baseline and intervention banks. At an exposure of $2^{36}$
per setting, the unweighted density reconstruction had minimum
eigenvalue $-0.2564$ and could not supply a modular logarithm.

There is an explicit parked collar charge on this same source. Let
$\chi$ be its frozen reference, $\pi=\chi_L\otimes\chi_R$, with reversal
parity included on the left, and $K_c=\log\pi-\log\chi$. Unpairing
preserves both marginals and gives the first variation
\[
 \delta\langle K_c\rangle
 =D(\pi\Vert\chi)+D(\chi\Vert\pi)
 =\delta S=-\eta\,\delta A|_V .
\]
The two relative entropies are $0.120213$ and $0.110893$ nats.
The volume-matching rotation preserves both marginals and has zero
entropy derivative. The parked Hamiltonian is scalar on each parity
block, so it conserves $K_c$. A flying probe tests the transport of
this charge. Let $P$ be the sharp unmarked-port event. If a conserved
operator $C$ commutes with $P$ and restricts there to $K_c$ up to a
constant, $K_c$ commutes with every return amplitude $P U(t)P$ on
that port and hence every return effect. Those effects span both full
parity blocks. A commuting head observable is scalar within each block
and therefore has zero unpairing derivative, a contradiction. In a
population calculation on the same prepared state, removing a probe
after two time units with its location unread changes the head charge
by $9.147\times10^{-5}$ nats. A conserved total stress carrying this
charge would require transport beyond an unchanged head observable
compatible with sharp launch-port detection.

The directional timings test whether this survey admits a common
propagation time. At the certified equilibrium measure the first arrival
of an excess and a vacancy separately on all twelve directed station
pairs. Include every miss at the six-unit deadline. Allow each probe
its own positive clock gain at each sender and independent additive
sender and receiver delays. A weighted sum of the twelve readings
with zero row and column sums eliminates all these delays. The four
weighted sender contributions are strictly positive for an excess and
strictly negative for a vacancy throughout the certified state
neighbourhood. Multiplication by positive gains preserves both signs.
Consequently the two sets of means cannot be one common table of
directed travel times under these calibrations, even allowing
nonreciprocal travel. Dispersion, first detection and censoring all
enter these observed means. A geometric signal extracted by some
other protocol has to predict independent directional measurements. The
same incompatibility holds for the first variation when the pairing is
weakened and the right rotation holds the original two-way survey volume
fixed. A second weighted sum eliminates endpoint delays on this tangent;
all four one-way excess terms and all four vacancy terms retain
opposite signs throughout the certified source enclosure when the
emitter gains are held fixed across the variation.

At the same six-unit deadline there are attainable state changes that
preserve all twelve two-way mean ranges exactly while changing the
parked exchange energy. The energy derivative per unit Hilbert--Schmidt
norm exceeds $0.024953$ on one such direction. Using three deadlines
instead gives 36 range means with a certified left inverse on the
31-dimensional traceless block algebra. This recovers a declared
matter observable. Assigning a geometrical curvature or a conserved
null stress to those data requires an independent physical law.

\paragraph{Independent recovery of the same area and a local current.}
At one deadline the two charge-resolved twelve-range maps together
have rank 24. On any open set of head states their outputs therefore
contain a product of open sets. A quantity reconstructed from either
one of the two mean tables alone must be locally constant if the two
values always agree. The conclusion changes when the clocks retain
the time distribution. At deadlines 2, 4 and 6, each individual charge
gives a rank-31 design on the traceless block space. Its fixed linear
inverse recovers the twelve prescribed pooled observables from that
charge alone. This identifies a common operational survey, with its
geometry prescription fixed before the source changes.

In separate acquisitions using each probe, every returning tick and
every miss by time 12 was counted. Each of the twelve sender--relay
pairs used \(2^{50}\) synthetic trials per preparation. A reference
bank of the same species fixed the local area and volume derivatives.
Two physically prepared quarter rotations in opposite directions
measured the volume-adjuster tangent by half their difference.
The vacancy-only estimates of the matched area slopes were
\(-0.017689\pm0.000594\) for ordinary exchange and
\(-0.009993\pm0.000178\) for unpairing. The excess-only values were
\(-0.018051\pm0.002943\) and \(-0.010101\pm0.000880\).
Simultaneous 99\% sampling bounds include both exposures and the
independent source calibrations of the geometric derivatives.
At \(2^{42}\) shots per setting the excess intervals include zero.
The agreement at higher exposure establishes a reproducible area
readout in the specified survey units.

The source also has an exactly conserved extension of its modular
charge during a flight. Two spatial eigenmodes with support on six
unmarked ports have zero amplitude on gateways and marked contacts.
Their orthogonal projector \(\Pi\) has diagonal \(1/3\) on those six
ports. For any stored parity-block observable \(K\), the full flight
Hamiltonian commutes with \(C=3\Pi\otimes K\), and a launch at port
5 compresses \(C\) to \(K\). The density at port \(x\) is
\(\{P_x,C\}/2\). Symmetrizing the native probability edge current
with \(C\) gives an antisymmetric current \(J\) with exact local
continuity. A source-operator calculation on edge \(4\to5\) gives
\(\partial_t\langle J\rangle(0)=-3\langle K\rangle/8\) for either
probe. Set \(K=-\log\chi_*\) from independent head tomography.
For every first variation at fixed survey volume this gives
\[
 \delta\,\partial_t\langle J_{4\to5}\rangle(0)
     =\tfrac38\eta\,\delta A|_V .
\]
Ordinary exchange changes this slope by \(-0.1535450\) nats per
squared exchange time per unit intervention. Unpairing changes it by
\(-0.0866648\), entirely from the collar part of \(K\).
The current here redistributes charge within a compact region with
zero total outflow. Its time derivative and the surveyed area are
distinct observables from null stress and spacetime curvature.

The launch charge and its conservation also admit different
intercell currents on the same native board. In each charge and
parity sector write \(H\) for the integer flight generator after
subtracting its scalar launch energy. For the span of monomials of degrees
zero and two through twenty, map the coefficients to the symmetric
launch block at port 5 and the symmetric part of the cross block
from port 5 to port 38. Each block has ten real coordinates.
The four integer twenty-dimensional minors, using twice the symmetric
cross block, are \(756,886,637,254\) modulo the prime 1009.
Thus each sector allows both blocks to be specified independently.
For the collar observable
\(K_c=\log(\chi_{*L}\otimes\chi_{*R})-\log\chi_*\),
set the launch block to \(\operatorname{Re}K_c\) and add
\(3\Pi\otimes i\operatorname{Im}K_c\) to restore the full Hermitian
launch charge. The cross block can have symmetric part
\(B=0\) or \(B=\pm\operatorname{Re}K_c\), with the same conservation
law in each case.

The only two-hop path from port 5 into another cell goes through
port 1 to port 38. The receiving cell's charge has initial second
time derivative \(-\langle B\rangle/4\) in native exchange units.
With \(B=\operatorname{Re}K_c\), raw unpairing changes this derivative
by approximately \(-0.05777\), with its sign certified at the true
equilibrium. Choosing the negative cross block reverses the sign
while giving exactly the same collar reading for every launch state. These interpolating operators
can have very large norm. Their role is an exact non-identification
result. A local coupling to the source action or an independently
specified transport instrument would distinguish physical stress
among the conserved extensions.

\paragraph{An independent, source-sensitive relay metric.}
A second ruler times a low-probability click on a sharp launch in
both link orientations. At fixed graph distance the leading arrival
coefficients on the enrolled links agree between excess and vacancy
after the two orientations are averaged. Refer the small-threshold
time to a blank path of the same hop order, then take the shortest
sum over relays. This gives a reciprocal metric on 960 ports, and
three-hop links can respond to unpairing. Four-hop links give a
nondegenerate Euclidean tetrahedron on the four original stations.
Hold its own measured volume fixed and calibrate on ordinary exchange.
In area units for this ruler the ordinary derivative lies between
\(-0.004923\) and \(-0.004869\). Unpairing increases entropy while
its area \emph{increases} by \((1.5935\text{--}1.6127)\times10^{-5}\)
per unit intervention. The predicted-to-measured entropy response
lies between \(-0.005871\) and \(-0.005732\); stationarity requires
one. This failure includes the certified equilibrium uncertainty.

The same relay data test whether ordinary calibration selects an
intercell flux. Use the three-hop direct length from port 0 to 39.
Its frozen gradient \(L\) is real symmetric because the length is
\((\operatorname{Tr}\chi F)^{-1/6}\) for a fixed real symmetric
arrival effect \(F\). On the original survey's constant-volume
ordinary and unpairing directions, denote length derivatives by
\(\ell'_o,\ell'_p\) and parked energy derivatives by \(E'_o,E'_p\).
Here \(E'_o>0.29756\), \(E'_p=0\) and
\(0.0357614<\ell'_p<0.0362044\). Subtract
\((\ell'_o/E'_o)E+cI\) from \(L\), with \(c\) chosen to center
the resulting observable \(M\) at the reference. Its reference
expectation and ordinary response both vanish, while its unpairing
response is \(\ell'_p\). A cross block \(aI+bE\) fits any specified
reference current slope and ordinary response and has zero unpairing
response. Adding or subtracting \(M\) preserves both calibrations
and the complete parked charge. The resulting conserved extensions
have opposite intercell responses to unpairing at fixed survey
volume. This ambiguity survives calibration on ordinary matter.

A second physical variation identifies information that all such sharp
leading-arrival rulers discard. In the odd parity block take imaginary
Hermitian variations \(Y\otimes Z/2\) and \(Z\otimes Y/2\), where
\(Y,Z\) are Pauli matrices. Both preserve each individual head state
and ordinary energy. The leading transition probability between sharp
ports pairs the head density with a real symmetric matrix because the
native Hamiltonian is real. It therefore cannot see either variation,
at any hop order. Their two-dimensional plane contains a unit tangent
to an actual constant-volume curve of the original survey. On this
tangent the collar charge increases by \(0.0003224\) to \(0.0003260\)
nats per unit Hilbert--Schmidt displacement. An independent enclosure
using the head logarithm gives a lower bound \(0.00029\). The entire
metric made from sharp leading-arrival probabilities is unchanged.
For example, center two uniform vertex balls on ports 0 and 39 in the
three-hop metric. At radii 0.75 and 1.5 blank-link units their
directional transport curvatures \(1-W_1/d\) are \(-0.70584\) and
\(-0.70689\) at the computed reference. Both have exactly zero
derivative along this phase curve. This excludes an interpretation
of these spatial observations alone as a response to pressureless
collar energy at nonzero coupling. A physical lapse, directional
pressure and the continuum interpretation of the balls require
independent measurements.

There is a direct way to recover the missing phase information.
Launch or receive on two adjacent blank ports with a relative
quarter-period phase. Two such apertures give independent leading
probability slopes on the two phase directions above. In the stated
basis their diagonal responses are \(-1/8\) and \(-9/64\), with
zero off-diagonal entries. Both carrier species resolve the phase.
Calibrate each aperture's threshold length on the full ordinary
modular response, then hold the calibration fixed. For unpairing,
observed length change divided by predicted length change is in
\([0.95429,0.98466]\) for one aperture and \([0.52665,0.54540]\)
for the other. The held phase change gives opposite signs to those
predicted for both apertures. Detecting the hidden correlations is
thus possible using independent optical records. Interpreting either
single threshold length as their universally calibrated response
fails these controls.

A single right-hand material rail distinguishes this selected state
from the original freely formed heads. The same native transfer
followed by a sharp first-port reading gives probability 0.020107
on the formed source and 0.507974 on the full stationary source.
Replacing the formed heads by independent heads with the same
marginals or dephasing the left reversal sign leaves the former
right-hand reading fixed. Thus the state-selection input is visible
in a separate local measurement.

A fifth unmarked station tests the geometry independently. Include
port 5 with the original four stations, rerun all twenty directed
flights at the same state, and refit the five clock gains. The four
nontrivial eigenvalues of the centered squared-distance Gram matrix
are $5.2623,4.6982,3.7321,3.4961$. The smallest is $0.6644$ of the
largest. The distances therefore require four Euclidean dimensions
for an exact embedding at this measurement scale. This test concerns
the proposed flat reconstruction; finite geodesic distances on a
curved three-space need not have a rank-three Euclidean Gram matrix.

The same state and gain at a four-unit timeout give gradient residual
$0.7264$ even after choosing the best new volume multiplier. In the
six-unit survey the four directed radar cycle ratios range from
0.98548 to 1.01539, whereas a static reciprocal distance with only
station clock gains requires one on every cycle. A selected finite
entropy maximum can coexist with this failure of the proposed
spatial reconstruction. To obtain a gravitational equation, a family
of common proper balls and their signed changes must be recovered
from the evolving physical records. The full probability weights and
held measurements are in
\path{code/matched_einstein_limit/source_implication/full_algebra/}.

\paragraph{Which area deficit contracts a proper ball?}
For a rest observer with nested geodesic balls before the cut locus,
proper radial thickness and the area of the shell obey \(dV=A\,dr\).
At matched enclosed volume \(v\), the radius is therefore
\(r(v)=\int_0^v dw/A(w)\). A positive bulk entropy variation that
reduces the proper area at every matched interior volume increases
the radius needed to enclose a given volume. Inverting the two
radius functions gives a smaller enclosed volume at a fixed proper
radius. In three spatial dimensions the leading curvature contrasts
satisfy \(\delta V|_r=(r/3)\delta A|_V+o(r^5)\). On four
supplied curved charts with nonzero sectional curvature, independent
simulated interior and surface counts give
\(3\Delta V/(r\Delta A)=0.99921\)--\(1.00069\) at radius \(1/16\),
up to the displayed rounding.

The finite version identifies what an observer must measure. Let
\(w_i\) be a common shell volume increment, \(a_i,b_i\) the
positive reference and perturbed areas, and \(s_i\) the regional
entropy contrast at the matched volume. Write
\(e_i=s_i+\eta(b_i-a_i)\). Let \(c\) be the measured difference
between the radial-clock contrast and the two sums of inferred shell
thicknesses. Then
\[
 r_1-r_0=c+\sum_i\frac{w_i(s_i-e_i)}{\eta a_i b_i}.
\]
Bounds on \(e_i\) and \(c\) give a quantitative contraction test.
They include shell discretization and, for finite changes about a
stationary reference, the nonlinear remainder.

The two-head experiment distinguishes these conditions. One
physical decorrelation raises the joint entropy. A rotation of the
right head about its own marginal axis leaves both marginals, the
spectrum, the mutual information and ordinary energy fixed.
The map from ordinary admixture, decorrelation and rotation to
parked energy, entropy and survey volume has Jacobian determinant
between \(-1.779\times10^{-6}\) and \(-1.763\times10^{-6}\).
Consequently the same entropy and energy changes admit smaller,
equal or larger survey volume. Scalar compensation at fixed survey
volume therefore supplies only one of the shell measurements.

\paragraph{Six clock stations and a transverse optical image.}
Bounded integer propagation of both probes encloses thirty directed
ranges at the full-head equilibrium. Five stations admit a round
three-sphere fit at inverse radius between 0.6221 and 0.6224 in
survey units. The five points surround the embedding origin. Hold
their clock gains fixed and enroll a sixth station: the six-point
cosine Gram has a negative uniform-direction Rayleigh quotient
throughout the fitted interval. Varying the curvature across either
sign gives no common three-dimensional space-form chart for these
six minimizing distances. On the spherical side the centered Gram
has five positive directions below inverse radius 0.65, and the
uniform direction is negative above it up to the diameter cutoff.
On the hyperbolic side a fourth negative direction, invertible
minors, and a hyperbolic chord Ptolemy inequality cover the low,
intermediate and high inverse radii respectively. The result
assumes a common chart for the distance comparisons.

Even the five stations constrain variable curvature.
Their centered Euclidean Gram has four eigenvalues exceeding 3.49,
with maximum squared distance below 9.03. A three-dimensional
tangent-space fit requires a change above 1.745 in some squared
distance. In a strongly convex normal ball with
\(R^2\sup|\mathrm{sec}|\le1/2\), radial Jacobi comparison bounds
normal-coordinate length distortion between \(11/12\) and \(12/11\):
these bounds follow from \(\sin(x)/x\) and \(\sinh(x)/x\) at
\(x^2\le1/2\). The squared-distance error is at most
\(9.03[(12/11)^2-1]<1.717\), a contradiction. This constrains the
observed shape at its measured scale. A refining detector protocol
can change the shape.

Two five-port material screens across the bridge provide an angular
image, with two launch biases and two independent centroid readings.
In the earliest transmitted signal one image component is
proportional to the connected correlation of the two stored heads.
The perpendicular component vanishes. At exposure four the image
has nonzero area for either probe. On the same launch ensemble and
at the same exposure, a separate instrument measures the native
energy of the receiving seam. Calibrate image-area change against
this energy change on the ordinary intervention. The held ratio of
observed to predicted image response is in \([0.85184,0.93919]\)
for a vacancy and \([-0.16242,-0.14329]\) for an excess. Both
enclosures include the exact stationary-source uncertainty.
The comparison uses no entropy estimator.
These are independent detections of a correlation affecting
propagation and energy. Relating this image to null focusing would
require proper transverse separations, affine ray parameters,
control of diffraction and shear, and the directional stress of
the same region.

\paragraph{Reciprocity of the two material cameras.}
Prepare the receiver from an independent time-reversed copy of the
source lottery, giving effect $X_\pi^{\mathsf T}$ in each head parity
block of density $X_\pi$. For the native transfer matrices,
$K_{ij}=K_{ji}^{\mathsf T}$. The transmission probability
$\sum_\pi\| (X_\pi^{1/2})^{\mathsf T} K_{ji} X_\pi^{1/2}\|_F^2$
is therefore identical in reverse, separately for each probe charge.
Three uniformly illuminated pixels facing five satisfy
$3p_{\rm forward}=5p_{\rm reverse}$. At four ticks the two-way
angular image-area gains at the enclosed equilibrium lie in
$[1.226068,1.286387]\times10^{-4}$ for vacancies and
$[0.720380,1.248067]\times10^{-8}$ for excesses, including joint
99\% counting uncertainty and the source localization error. A held coin rotation breaks reciprocity at the frozen
receiver. At every nonzero real algebraic exposure, the native
reciprocity equations have full column rank on a source neighbourhood.
Even holding all six projected station lengths and four clock gains
fixed leaves a source family of full affine span. Thus only zero fixed
terminal head effects are reciprocal throughout that family. The matched receiver measures angular images;
a transverse proper area and an affine ruler require independent
calibration.%
\footnote{\href{https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/tree/main/code/matched_einstein_limit/source_implication/full_geometry/station_balls/reciprocal_screen}{Two-way material counts, source rotation and exact rank proof.}}

There is also a reciprocal acquisition for an unknown source.
Store the arrived pair of records, draw an independent copy from the
same preparation, and compare each pair locally with a native Bell
coincidence. A resolved reversal parity selects the matching block.
The two coincidences accept a fraction
\[
 P_{ji}(X,Y)=\tfrac14\sum_\pi
   \operatorname{tr}(Y_\pi^{\mathsf T} K_{ji} X_\pi K_{ji}^\dagger).
\]
When $Y=X$ this is symmetric under exchange of the two pixels at every
flight time. The same fixed controls work on the rotated source. Two
independent preparations and enrolled storage and comparison operations
supply the second argument. An alternative procedure estimates the
analyzer from the shifts and boundary currents of the traveling load
on three mounts. Those readings leave the source heads unmeasured.
Both procedures calibrate reciprocal material transmissions. The two
probe charges give different angular image areas, and proper
transverse area requires its own measurement.%
\footnote{\href{https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/tree/main/code/matched_einstein_limit/source_implication/full_geometry/station_balls/reciprocal_screen/live_reference}{Live-reference comparison and controls};
\href{https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/tree/main/code/matched_einstein_limit/source_implication/full_algebra/correlation_rank/spatial_cuts/chart_transport/adaptive_optics}{Independent regional analyzer calibration}.}

A second source variation keeps the same six surveyed lengths, the
individual head marginals and the parity weights. Along this curve the
clock gains vary, while the surveyed area $A$ and volume $V$ stay fixed.
The raw ranges have the form $r(s)=r\cosh s+v r\sinh s$, where $v$
changes sign between two station groups. Stationarity cancels the odd
term of the frozen reference modular charge. Homogeneity of the area
and volume then gives
\[
 \Delta\langle K_*\rangle=(3\lambda V-2\eta A)(\cosh s-1),\qquad
 -237.543<3\lambda V-2\eta A<-236.819
\]
in nats. Since
$\Delta S=\Delta\langle K_*\rangle-D(\rho(s)\Vert\rho_*)$, entropy
decreases for every sufficiently small nonzero $s$. Its first
derivative vanishes at the reference. This distinguishes first-order
entropy stationarity from an identification of entropy with the
surveyed boundary area at finite displacement.

\paragraph{When the four observers see a contraction.}
Two unconstrained interventions on the prepared equilibrium let the
clocks decide the sign of the volume change. The ordinary unread write
raises parked exchange energy from $0.20244$ to $0.50000$ and increases
entropy by $0.190$ nats. The observers reconstruct a smaller
tetrahedron from its round-trip times. Its volume moves from
$[2.76290,2.76405]$ to $[2.73713,2.73826]$. Replacing the right record
with an independent draw preserves the ordinary energy and increases
entropy by $0.111$ nats. The reconstructed volume moves to
$[2.75500,2.75618]$. The intervals include simultaneous 99\% counting
uncertainty across all preparations. Each route retains timed-out
signals. Separate runs read the two local Pauli outcomes and reversal
parity to determine energy and entropy. Neither intervention selects
the final volume.

There is a finite implication for every state $X$ measured with these
clocks. Let $r_j$ denote the twelve directed mean times in range units,
and use a star for the exact independently calibrated stationary
source. The six corrected lengths satisfy $\log\ell=L\log r$ with a
fixed linear map $L$ given by the clock-gain correction. Set
$w_j=r_j^*(\eta\,\partial_jA-\lambda\,\partial_jV)_*$ and
$b_{ij}=w_{ij}+w_{ji}$. The interval gradients give
$14.71<w_j<23.76$ and $236.8196<B=\sum_e b_e<237.5429$ nats.
Area and volume depend on the six lengths, hence $w=L^{\mathsf T}b$.
Indeed scaling the two opposite times together changes just the log
length of their common edge. With $z_j=\log(r_j/r_j^*)$, stationarity
and the affinity of each timing mean in the preparation give the
finite frozen modular work $-\sum_jw_j(e^{z_j}-1)$.
The relative entropy identity gives
\[
 S_*-S(X)=D(X\Vert X_*)+\sum_jw_j(e^{z_j}-1-z_j)
          +\sum_e b_e\log(\ell_e/\ell_e^*).
\]
An entropy increase therefore forces the weighted geometric mean
$\prod_e(\ell_e/\ell_e^*)^{b_e/B}$ below one. A volume change also
depends on shape. Increasing both log times on edge 01 and decreasing
both on edge 02 at the same speed gives $V'>0.7494$ and $S'>16.95$.
The rank-twelve correlation directions realize these derivatives even
when an independent parity-energy increment is included. This control
distinguishes the measured contraction after a specified write from a
universal response to any increment of the parked energy. Testing
gravity requires a common proper geometry and an independently
measured regional source across probes and scales.%
\footnote{\href{https://github.com/FloatingPragma/observer-patch-holography/tree/main/code/matched_einstein_limit/source_implication/full_geometry/station_balls/reciprocal_screen/range_contraction}{Complete timing histograms, separate tomography, finite inequality and shape control.}}

\paragraph{What independent observers agree on after free formation.}
Two more clock stations enroll against the original four. Fifteen
station quartets observe the same three preparations, with every
timed-out signal retained. The ordinary write contracts twelve
reconstructed tetrahedra and expands three. Weakening the correlation
contracts seven and expands eight, at simultaneous 99\% coverage.
Each quartet admits a Euclidean tetrahedron. The full squared-distance
matrix has five positive centered Gram eigenvalues on every stock.
These finite volumes belong to their respective surveys. Calling them
cells of a common flat three-space would add a geometric assumption.

A moving excess can also deliver ordinary exchange energy to the
original station. An observer intercepts a pair of incoming ports
after free flight. The native write converts their exchange-odd
projector exactly into the odd reversal-energy projector of the
attached record. The controller retains a declared reference
preparation on a miss. The increased stored energy on the target
survey is thus predictable from the separately observed incident
occupation and exchange parity. On the sharp external launch the
net energy increases by $[0.045618,0.045622]$ at exposure one and the
original volume changes by $[-0.005107,-0.004951]$. Capture, restoration
of the host and repeated inquiries are part of this protocol.

A separate free encounter forms correlations between two parked
records. Removing the mobile without reading its position leaves a
repeatable source. Pool the directed ranges reciprocally as
$D_{ij}=\sqrt{r_{ij}r_{ji}}$ for each charge. Compare
$(D_{ij}D_{kl})/(D_{ik}D_{jl})$ before and after replacing one of
the source marginals with an independent draw. All endpoint gains
cancel even when recalibrated separately on each stock. Constant
edge calibrations also cancel from the ratio of changes. On eighteen
of the forty-five quartet comparisons, one probe records an increase
where the other records a decrease. This excludes a single static
multiplicative change of ranges for these two instruments. An independent
reciprocal image test on the formed stock also gives opposite signs for
the determinant of the correlation response relative to the
ordinary-energy response. The central estimates are $-1.276$ and
$+3.886$. A fixed change of image coordinates preserves these signs,
as does a nonzero scalar change of sensitivity in two image
dimensions. These comparisons use measurements on signal routes.

One can keep a moving source present during the clock experiment.
Two initially separated material loads evolve freely on a 20-cell
host. Separate cohorts continue unconditionally or enroll a detected
pair encounter and report every failed herald. Three locally
